    SELECT "c"."Id", "c"."CategoryId", "c"."CreatedAt", "c"."CreatedById", "c"."Description", "c"."MolecularFormula", "c"."Name", "c"."ReviewComments", "c"."Status", "c"."Synonym", "c"."UpdatedAt", "c0"."Id" AS "Id0", "c0"."Description" AS "Description0", "c0"."Name" AS "Name0", "a"."Id" AS "Id1", "a"."AccessFailedCount", "a"."ConcurrencyStamp", "a"."CreatedAt" AS "CreatedAt0", "a"."Email", "a"."EmailConfirmed", "a"."FullName", "a"."IsActive", "a"."LockoutEnabled", "a"."LockoutEnd", "a"."NormalizedEmail", "a"."NormalizedUserName", "a"."PasswordHash", "a"."PhoneNumber", "a"."PhoneNumberConfirmed", "a"."SecurityStamp", "a"."TwoFactorEnabled", "a"."UserName"

    LIMIT 1
) AS "s"

) AS "s0" ON "s"."Id" = "s0"."CompoundId"
